(() => {
    const header = document.querySelector("[data-site-header]");
    const navToggle = document.querySelector("[data-nav-toggle]");
    const mobileMenu = document.querySelector("[data-mobile-menu]");

    if (header && navToggle && mobileMenu) {
        const setMenuOpen = (open) => {
            navToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(open));
            navToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", open ? "Tutup menu navigasi" : "Buka menu navigasi");
            mobileMenu.hidden = !open;

            if (open) {
                const firstLink = mobileMenu.querySelector("a[href]");
                if (firstLink) {
                    firstLink.focus();
                }
            }
        };

        navToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const isOpen = navToggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") === "true";
            setMenuOpen(!isOpen);
        });

        mobileMenu.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
            if (event.target.closest("a")) {
                setMenuOpen(false);
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
            if (event.key === "Escape" && navToggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") === "true") {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                navToggle.focus();
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
            if (!header.contains(event.target) && navToggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") === "true") {
                setMenuOpen(false);
            }
        });

        const desktopQuery = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1025px)");
        desktopQuery.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
            if (event.matches && navToggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") === "true") {
                setMenuOpen(false);
            }
        });
    }

    const navLinks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-nav-id]"));
    const navSections = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-nav-match]"));

    if (document.body.dataset.page === "beranda" && navLinks.length && navSections.length) {
        let ticking = false;

        const setActiveNav = (id) => {
            navLinks.forEach((link) => {
                const isActive = link.dataset.navId === id;
                link.classList.toggle("is-active", isActive);

                if (isActive) {
                    link.setAttribute("aria-current", "location");
                } else {
                    link.removeAttribute("aria-current");
                }
            });
        };

        const updateActiveNav = () => {
            const marker = window.scrollY + 140;
            let activeId = navSections[0].dataset.navMatch;

            navSections.forEach((section) => {
                const top = section.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY;

                if (top <= marker) {
                    activeId = section.dataset.navMatch;
                }
            });

            const nearBottom = window.innerHeight + window.scrollY >= document.documentElement.scrollHeight - 4;

            if (nearBottom) {
                activeId = "aspirasi";
            }

            setActiveNav(activeId);
            ticking = false;
        };

        window.addEventListener("scroll", () => {
            if (!ticking) {
                window.requestAnimationFrame(updateActiveNav);
                ticking = true;
            }
        }, { passive: true });

        window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
            if (!ticking) {
                window.requestAnimationFrame(updateActiveNav);
                ticking = true;
            }
        }, { passive: true });

        updateActiveNav();
    }

    const explorer = document.querySelector("[data-sekbid]");

    if (explorer) {
        const tabs = Array.from(explorer.querySelectorAll("[data-sekbid-tab]"));
        const panels = Array.from(explorer.querySelectorAll('[role="tabpanel"]'));

        if (tabs.length && panels.length) {
            const selectTab = (index, moveFocus = false) => {
                tabs.forEach((tab, tabIndex) => {
                    const selected = tabIndex === index;
                    tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(selected));
                    tab.tabIndex = selected ? 0 : -1;

                    if (selected) {
                        if (moveFocus) {
                            tab.focus();
                        }

                        tab.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", inline: "nearest" });
                    }
                });

                panels.forEach((panel, panelIndex) => {
                    panel.hidden = panelIndex !== index;
                });
            };

            tabs.forEach((tab, index) => {
                tab.addEventListener("click", () => selectTab(index));
            });

            explorer.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
                const currentIndex = tabs.indexOf(document.activeElement);

                if (currentIndex === -1) {
                    return;
                }

                let nextIndex = null;

                switch (event.key) {
                    case "ArrowRight":
                    case "ArrowDown":
                        nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % tabs.length;
                        break;
                    case "ArrowLeft":
                    case "ArrowUp":
                        nextIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length;
                        break;
                    case "Home":
                        nextIndex = 0;
                        break;
                    case "End":
                        nextIndex = tabs.length - 1;
                        break;
                    default:
                        return;
                }

                event.preventDefault();
                selectTab(nextIndex, true);
            });
        }
    }

    const aspirationForm = document.querySelector("[data-aspiration-form]");

    if (aspirationForm) {
        const submitButton = aspirationForm.querySelector("[data-submit-button]");
        const submitLabel = aspirationForm.querySelector("[data-submit-label]");

        aspirationForm.addEventListener("submit", () => {
            if (!aspirationForm.checkValidity()) {
                return;
            }

            if (submitButton) {
                submitButton.disabled = true;
                submitButton.classList.add("is-loading");
                submitButton.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
            }

            if (submitLabel) {
                submitLabel.textContent = "Mengirim...";
            }
        });

        window.addEventListener("pageshow", () => {
            if (submitButton) {
                submitButton.disabled = false;
                submitButton.classList.remove("is-loading");
                submitButton.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
            }

            if (submitLabel) {
                submitLabel.textContent = "Kirim aspirasi";
            }
        });
    }

    const successCard = document.querySelector("[data-success]");
    const errorSummary = document.querySelector("[data-error-summary]");

    if (successCard) {
        successCard.focus();
    }

    if (errorSummary) {
        errorSummary.focus();
    }
})();

FILE: public/js/osis.js
====================================================================================================
/* Cegah form terkirim dua kali karena klik ganda.
   Form tetap berfungsi normal kalau JavaScript dimatikan. */
const forms = document.querySelectorAll('form[data-submit-once]');

forms.forEach((form) => {
    const submitButton = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');

    if (submitButton) {
        submitButton.dataset.label = submitButton.textContent;
    }

    form.addEventListener('submit', (event) => {
        if (form.dataset.submitting === 'true') {
            event.preventDefault();
            return;
        }

        const button = event.submitter || submitButton;

        if (!button) {
            return;
        }

        form.dataset.submitting = 'true';
        form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
        button.disabled = true;
        button.textContent = 'Menyimpan…';
    });
});

/* Kembalikan tombol saat halaman dipulihkan dari cache browser. */
window.addEventListener('pageshow', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('form[data-submit-once]').forEach((form) => {
        const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');

        delete form.dataset.submitting;
        form.removeAttribute('aria-busy');

        if (button && button.dataset.label) {
            button.disabled = false;
            button.textContent = button.dataset.label;
        }
    });
});
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-foto-dropzone]').forEach((dropzone) => {
        const input = dropzone.querySelector('[data-foto-input]');
        const preview = dropzone.querySelector('[data-foto-preview]');
        if (!input || !preview) return;

        const showFile = (file) => {
            if (!file || !file.type.startsWith('image/')) return;
            preview.src = URL.createObjectURL(file);
            preview.hidden = false;
            const copy = dropzone.querySelector('.foto-upload-copy');
            if (copy) copy.innerHTML = '<strong>Foto siap diunggah</strong><br><small>' + file.name.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])) + '</small>';
            const hapusFoto = document.getElementById('hapus_foto');
            if (hapusFoto) hapusFoto.value = '0';
            const fotoLama = document.getElementById('foto-preview-box');
            if (fotoLama) fotoLama.style.display = 'none';
        };

        input.addEventListener('change', () => showFile(input.files[0]));
        ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach((eventName) => dropzone.addEventListener(eventName, (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.add('is-dragging');
        }));
        ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach((eventName) => dropzone.addEventListener(eventName, (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('is-dragging');
        }));
        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (event) => {
            const file = event.dataTransfer.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            const transfer = new DataTransfer();
            transfer.items.add(file);
            input.files = transfer.files;
            showFile(file);
        });
    });
});

FILE: resources/views/auth/reset-password.blade.php

<x-guest-layout>
    <form method="POST" action="{{ route('password.store') }}">
        @csrf

        <!-- Password Reset Token -->
        <input type="hidden" name="token" value="{{ $request->route('token') }}">

        <!-- Email Address -->
        <div>
            <x-input-label for="email" :value="__('Email')" />
            <x-text-input id="email" class="block mt-1 w-full" type="email" name="email" :value="old('email', $request->email)" required autofocus autocomplete="username" />
            <x-input-error :messages="$errors->get('email')" class="mt-2" />
        </div>

        <!-- Password -->
        <div class="mt-4">
            <x-input-label for="password" :value="__('Password')" />
            <x-text-input id="password" class="block mt-1 w-full" type="password" name="password" required autocomplete="new-password" />
            <x-input-error :messages="$errors->get('password')" class="mt-2" />
        </div>

        <!-- Confirm Password -->
        <div class="mt-4">
            <x-input-label for="password_confirmation" :value="__('Confirm Password')" />

            <x-text-input id="password_confirmation" class="block mt-1 w-full"
                                type="password"
                                name="password_confirmation" required autocomplete="new-password" />

            <x-input-error :messages="$errors->get('password_confirmation')" class="mt-2" />
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center justify-end mt-4">
            <x-primary-button>
                {{ __('Reset Password') }}
            </x-primary-button>
        </div>
    </form>
</x-guest-layout>

            <div class="bph-block">
                <h3 class="subheading">Badan Pengurus Harian (BPH)</h3>

                @php
                    $bphFromDb = isset($osisList)
                        ? $osisList->whereIn('jabatan', ['Ketua Umum', 'Wakil Ketua', 'Sekretaris', 'Bendahara'])
                            ->sortBy(function ($o) {
                                $urutan = ['Ketua Umum' => 1, 'Wakil Ketua' => 2, 'Sekretaris' => 3, 'Bendahara' => 4];
                                return $urutan[$o->jabatan] ?? 99;
                            })
                            ->values()

                @if ($bphFromDb->isNotEmpty())
                    <div class="bph-grid">

                                @if ($person->foto)
                                    <span class="bph-avatar" aria-hidden="true"><img src="{{ asset('storage/osis/' . $person->foto) }}" alt="" width="40" height="40"></span>

                                    <span class="bph-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{ mb_substr($person->nama, 0, 1) }}</span>
                                @endif
                                <div>
                                    <p class="bph-role">{{ $person->jabatan }}</p>
                                    <p class="bph-name">{{ $person->nama }}</p>
                                </div>
                            </article>
                        @endforeach
                    </div>
                @else
                    <div class="empty-state">
                        <strong>Data pengurus belum tersedia.</strong>
                        <p>Nama dan jabatan akan tampil di sini setelah ditambahkan melalui panel admin.</p>
                    </div>
                @endif
            </div>

            <div class="members-block" id="anggota">
                <div class="section-head section-head--stacked members-head">
                    <div class="members-head-row">
                        <div>
                            <p class="section-kicker">Data Terdaftar</p>
                            <h3 class="subheading">Daftar Anggota OSIS</h3>
                            <p class="section-lede">Anggota resmi yang tercatat di dalam sistem database OSIS.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                @if (isset($osisList) && $osisList->isNotEmpty())
                    <div class="members-grid">
                        @foreach ($osisList as $item)
                            <article class="member-card">
                                <span class="member-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{ mb_substr($item->nama, 0, 1) }}</span>
                                <div class="member-info">
                                    <span class="member-sekbid">{{ $item->sekbid }}</span>
                                    <h4 class="member-name">{{ $item->nama }}</h4>
                                    <p class="member-meta">ID {{ $item->id_osis }} &middot; {{ $item->kelas }} &middot; {{ $item->jurusan }}</p>
                                </div>
                            </article>
                        @endforeach
                    </div>
                @else
                    <div class="empty-state">
                        <strong>Belum ada anggota di database.</strong>
                        <p>Anggota OSIS yang ditambahkan melalui panel admin akan langsung tampil di sini.</p>
                    </div>
                @endif
            </div>

                                <td class="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-right text-sm">
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
                                        <a href="{{ route('osis.edit', $item->id) }}"
                                           class="font-medium text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 dark:hover:text-indigo-300">
                                            Edit
                                        </a>

                                        <form method="POST"
                                              action="{{ route('osis.destroy', $item->id) }}"
                                              onsubmit="return confirm('Hapus anggota ini? Tindakan ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.')">
                                            @csrf
                                            @method('DELETE')
                                            <button type="submit"
                                                    aria-label="Hapus {{ $item->nama }}"
                                                    class="font-medium text-red-600 hover:text-red-500 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:text-red-300">
                                                Hapus
                                            </button>
                                        </form>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        @endforeach
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        @endif
    </div>
</div>

FILE: resources/views/components/modal.blade.php
====================================================================================================
@props([
    'name',
    'show' => false,
    'maxWidth' => '2xl'
])

@php
$maxWidth = [
    'sm' => 'sm:max-w-sm',
    'md' => 'sm:max-w-md',
    'lg' => 'sm:max-w-lg',
    'xl' => 'sm:max-w-xl',
    '2xl' => 'sm:max-w-2xl',
][$maxWidth];
@endphp

<div
    x-data="{
        show: @js($show),
        focusables() {
            // All focusable element types...
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]
                // All non-disabled elements...
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))
        },
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },
    }"
    x-init="$watch('show', value => {
        if (value) {
            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');
            {{ $attributes->has('focusable') ? 'setTimeout(() => firstFocusable().focus(), 100)' : '' }}
        } else {
            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');
        }
    })"
    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = true : null"
    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = false : null"
    x-on:close.stop="show = false"
    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"
    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"
    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"
    x-show="show"
    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"
    style="display: {{ $show ? 'block' : 'none' }};"
>
    <div
        x-show="show"
        class="fixed inset-0 transform transition-all"
        x-on:click="show = false"
        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"
        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"
        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"
        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"
    >
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500 dark:bg-gray-900 opacity-75"></div>
    </div>

    <div
        x-show="show"
        class="mb-6 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow-xl transform transition-all sm:w-full {{ $maxWidth }} sm:mx-auto"
        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
    >
        {{ $slot }}
    </div>
</div>

        document.getElementById('image')?.addEventListener('change', function () {
            const preview = document.getElementById('event-image-preview');
            const file = this.files?.[0];
            if (!preview || !file) return;

        <div class="mx-auto max-w-3xl sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <form method="POST" enctype="multipart/form-data" action="{{ route('events.update', $event) }}" class="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-800">
                @include('events._form', ['event' => $event])
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>
</x-app-layout>

[]) : '') }}</textarea>

                <img src="{{ asset($news->image) }}" alt="Foto {{ $news->title }}" class="h-20 w-32 rounded-md border border-gray-200 object-cover dark:border-gray-700">

        @error('image') <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-red-600">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    const newsTitle = document.getElementById('title');
    const newsSlug = document.getElementById('slug');
    const slugifyNewsTitle = (value) => value.toLowerCase().normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '').slice(0, 180);
    newsTitle?.addEventListener('input', () => {
        if (newsSlug) newsSlug.value = slugifyNewsTitle(newsTitle.value);
    });
</script>

<div class="mt-8 flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-3 border-t border-gray-200 pt-5 dark:border-gray-700">
    <a href="{{ route('dashboard') }}" class="inline-flex items-center rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:border-gray-600 dark:text-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700">Batal</a>
    <button type="submit" class="inline-flex items-center rounded-md bg-gray-800 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-200 dark:text-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-white">{{ $news ? 'Simpan perubahan' : 'Tambah berita' }}</button>
</div>

@extends('layouts.osis')

@section('title', 'Tambah Anggota OSIS')

@section('content')
    <div class="page-head">
        <div class="page-head__text">
            <h1>Tambah anggota</h1>
            <p class="lede">Catat anggota baru. Kolom bertanda <span class="req">*</span> wajib diisi.</p>
        </div>
    </div>

    @if (session('success'))
        <div class="alert alert--success" role="status">
            {{ session('success') }}
        </div>
    @endif

    @if ($errors->any())
        <div class="alert alert--error" role="alert">
            <p class="alert__title">Ada {{ $errors->count() }} isian yang perlu diperbaiki.</p>
            <ul>
                @foreach ($errors->all() as $error)
                    <li>{{ $error }}</li>
                @endforeach
            </ul>
        </div>
    @endif

    <form class="form-card" action="{{ route('osis.store') }}" method="POST" enctype="multipart/form-data" data-submit-once>
        @csrf

        <div class="field">
            <label for="id_osis">ID OSIS <span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label>
            <input
                type="text"
                id="id_osis"
                name="id_osis"
                value="{{ old('id_osis') }}"
                maxlength="50"
                placeholder="Contoh: OSIS-001"
                required
                @if ($errors->has('id_osis')) aria-invalid="true" aria-describedby="id_osis-error" @endif
            >
            @error('id_osis')
                <p class="field-error" id="id_osis-error">{{ $message }}</p>
            @enderror
        </div>

        <div class="field">

            <a class="btn btn--ghost" href="{{ route('dashboard') }}">Batal</a>
        </div>
    </form>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const btnHapus = document.getElementById('btn-hapus-foto');
            if (btnHapus) {
                btnHapus.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    if (confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus foto ini?')) {
                        const hapusInput = document.getElementById('hapus_foto');
                        if (hapusInput) hapusInput.value = '1';
                        const previewBox = document.getElementById('foto-preview-box');
                        if (previewBox) previewBox.style.display = 'none';
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    </script>
@endsection

            <x-input-error class="mt-2" :messages="$errors->get('name')" />
        </div>

        <div>
            <x-input-label for="email" :value="__('Email')" />
            <x-text-input id="email" name="email" type="email" class="mt-1 block w-full" :value="old('email', $user->email)" required autocomplete="username" />
            <x-input-error class="mt-2" :messages="$errors->get('email')" />

            @if ($user instanceof \Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail && ! $user->hasVerifiedEmail())
                <div>

                        {{ __('Your email address is unverified.') }}

                        <button form="send-verification" class="underline text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-900 dark:hover:text-gray-100 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 dark:focus:ring-offset-gray-800">
                            {{ __('Click here to re-send the verification email.') }}
                        </button>
                    </p>

                    @if (session('status') === 'verification-link-sent')
                        <p class="mt-2 font-medium text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">
                            {{ __('A new verification link has been sent to your email address.') }}
                        </p>

                </div>
            @endif
        </div>

            @if (session('status') === 'profile-updated')
                <p
                    x-data="{ show: true }"

                    x-init="setTimeout(() => show = false, 2000)"
                    class="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400"
                >{{ __('Saved.') }}</p>
            @endif
        </div>
    </form>
</section>

    Route::get('panel-oska', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'create'])
        ->name('login');

    Route::post('panel-oska', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'store']);

    Route::get('forgot-password', [PasswordResetLinkController::class, 'create'])
        ->name('password.request');

    Route::post('forgot-password', [PasswordResetLinkController::class, 'store'])
        ->name('password.email');

    Route::get('reset-password/{token}', [NewPasswordController::class, 'create'])
        ->name('password.reset');

    Route::post('reset-password', [NewPasswordController::class, 'store'])
        ->name('password.store');
});

Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::get('verify-email', EmailVerificationPromptController::class)
        ->name('verification.notice');

    Route::get('verify-email/{id}/{hash}', VerifyEmailController::class)
        ->middleware(['signed', 'throttle:6,1'])
        ->name('verification.verify');

    Route::post('email/verification-notification', [EmailVerificationNotificationController::class, 'store'])
        ->middleware('throttle:6,1')
        ->name('verification.send');

    Route::get('confirm-password', [ConfirmablePasswordController::class, 'show'])
        ->name('password.confirm');

    Route::post('confirm-password', [ConfirmablePasswordController::class, 'store']);

    Route::put('password', [PasswordController::class, 'update'])->name('password.update');

    Route::post('logout', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'destroy'])
        ->name('logout');
});

<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\AspirationController;
use App\Http\Controllers\EventController;
use App\Http\Controllers\HomeController;
use App\Http\Controllers\NewsAdminController;
use App\Http\Controllers\NewsController;
use App\Http\Controllers\OsisController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ProfileController;
use App\Models\Event;
use App\Models\Aspiration;
use App\Models\Osis;
use App\Models\News;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::get('/', [HomeController::class, 'index'])->name('beranda');

Route::get('/aspirasi', [AspirationController::class, 'index'])->name('aspirasi.index');
Route::post('/aspirasi', [AspirationController::class, 'store'])
    ->middleware('throttle:5,1')
    ->name('aspirasi.store');

Route::get('/berita/{slug}', [NewsController::class, 'show'])->name('berita.show');

Route::get('/osis', fn () => redirect('/#anggota'));

Route::get('/dashboard', function (Request $request) {
    if (! $request->user()->isAdmin()) {
        return redirect()->route('beranda');
    }

    return view('dashboard', [
        'totalAnggota' => Osis::count(),
        'totalSekbid' => Osis::query()->distinct()->count('sekbid'),
        'anggotaList' => Osis::latest()->get(),
        'agendaList' => Event::query()->orderByDesc('start_date')->get(),
        'totalAgendaMendatang' => Event::upcoming()->count(),
        'newsList' => News::query()->orderByDesc('date')->get(),
        'aspirationList' => Aspiration::query()->latest()->get(),
    ]);
})->middleware('auth')->name('dashboard');

Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'edit'])->name('profile.edit');
    Route::patch('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'update'])->name('profile.update');
    Route::delete('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'destroy'])->name('profile.destroy');
});

Route::middleware(['auth', 'admin'])->group(function () {
    Route::get('/dashboard/berita/create', [NewsAdminController::class, 'create'])->name('news.create');
    Route::post('/dashboard/berita', [NewsAdminController::class, 'store'])->name('news.store');
    Route::get('/dashboard/berita/{news}/edit', [NewsAdminController::class, 'edit'])->name('news.edit');
    Route::put('/dashboard/berita/{news}', [NewsAdminController::class, 'update'])->name('news.update');
    Route::delete('/dashboard/berita/{news}', [NewsAdminController::class, 'destroy'])->name('news.destroy');

    Route::get('/agenda/create', [EventController::class, 'create'])->name('events.create');
    Route::post('/agenda', [EventController::class, 'store'])->name('events.store');
    Route::get('/agenda/{event}/edit', [EventController::class, 'edit'])->name('events.edit');
    Route::put('/agenda/{event}', [EventController::class, 'update'])->name('events.update');
    Route::delete('/agenda/{event}', [EventController::class, 'destroy'])->name('events.destroy');

    Route::get('/osis/create', [OsisController::class, 'create'])->name('osis.create');
    Route::post('/osis', [OsisController::class, 'store'])->name('osis.store');
    Route::get('/osis/{id}/edit', [OsisController::class, 'edit'])->name('osis.edit');
    Route::put('/osis/{id}', [OsisController::class, 'update'])->name('osis.update');
    Route::delete('/osis/{id}', [OsisController::class, 'destroy'])->name('osis.destroy');
});

require __DIR__.'/auth.php';

FILE: tests/Feature/AspirationTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class AspirationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private function validData(): array
    {
        return [
            'kategori' => 'Fasilitas',
            'pesan' => 'Mohon tambah tempat sampah di koridor lantai dua agar kelas tetap bersih.',
            'nama' => '',
        ];
    }

    public function test_guest_can_open_aspiration_form(): void
    {
        $this->get('/aspirasi')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('Kirim aspirasi')
            ->assertSee('Sebelum mengirim');
    }

    public function test_guest_can_submit_aspiration(): void
    {
        $data = $this->validData();
        $data['nama'] = 'Siswa Uji';

        $this->post('/aspirasi', $data)
            ->assertRedirect('/aspirasi')
            ->assertSessionHas('success');

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('aspirations', [
            'kategori' => 'Fasilitas',
            'pesan' => $data['pesan'],
            'nama' => 'Siswa Uji',
        ]);
    }

    public function test_aspiration_can_be_sent_without_name(): void
    {
        $this->post('/aspirasi', $this->validData())
            ->assertRedirect('/aspirasi')
            ->assertSessionHas('success');

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('aspirations', [
            'kategori' => 'Fasilitas',
            'nama' => null,
        ]);
    }

    public function test_category_and_message_are_required(): void
    {
        $this->post('/aspirasi', [
            'kategori' => '',
            'pesan' => '',
        ])
            ->assertSessionHasErrors(['kategori', 'pesan']);

        $this->assertDatabaseCount('aspirations', 0);
    }

    public function test_message_has_minimum_length(): void
    {
        $this->post('/aspirasi', [
            'kategori' => 'Akademik',
            'pesan' => 'Terlalu singkat.',
        ])->assertSessionHasErrors('pesan');

        $this->assertDatabaseCount('aspirations', 0);
    }

    public function test_category_must_be_available(): void
    {
        $this->post('/aspirasi', [
            'kategori' => 'Kategori Palsu',
            'pesan' => 'Mohon tambah tempat sampah di koridor lantai dua agar kelas tetap bersih.',
        ])->assertSessionHasErrors('kategori');

        $this->assertDatabaseCount('aspirations', 0);
    }
}

FILE: tests/Feature/Auth/AuthenticationTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class AuthenticationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_login_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $response = $this->get(route('login', absolute: false));

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_admin_can_authenticate_using_the_login_screen(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        $response = $this->post(route('login', absolute: false), [
            'email' => $user->email,
            'password' => 'password',
        ]);

        $this->assertAuthenticated();
        $response->assertRedirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    public function test_non_admin_users_can_not_authenticate(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create(['role' => User::ROLE_USER]);

        $response = $this->from(route('login', absolute: false))->post(route('login', absolute: false), [
            'email' => $user->email,
            'password' => 'password',
        ]);

        $this->assertGuest();
        $response->assertRedirect(route('login', absolute: false));
        $response->assertSessionHasErrors('email');
    }

    public function test_users_can_not_authenticate_with_invalid_password(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        $this->post(route('login', absolute: false), [
            'email' => $user->email,
            'password' => 'wrong-password',
        ]);

        $this->assertGuest();
    }

    public function test_users_can_logout(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/logout');

        $this->assertGuest();
        $response->assertRedirect('/');
    }
}

FILE: tests/Feature/Auth/EmailVerificationTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\URL;
use Tests\TestCase;

class EmailVerificationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_email_verification_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->unverified()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/verify-email');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_email_can_be_verified(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->unverified()->create();

        Event::fake();

        $verificationUrl = URL::temporarySignedRoute(
            'verification.verify',
            now()->addMinutes(60),
            ['id' => $user->id, 'hash' => sha1($user->email)]
        );

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get($verificationUrl);

        Event::assertDispatched(Verified::class);
        $this->assertTrue($user->fresh()->hasVerifiedEmail());
        $response->assertRedirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }

    public function test_email_is_not_verified_with_invalid_hash(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->unverified()->create();

        $verificationUrl = URL::temporarySignedRoute(
            'verification.verify',
            now()->addMinutes(60),
            ['id' => $user->id, 'hash' => sha1('wrong-email')]
        );

        $this->actingAs($user)->get($verificationUrl);

        $this->assertFalse($user->fresh()->hasVerifiedEmail());
    }
}

<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PasswordConfirmationTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_confirm_password_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/confirm-password');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_password_can_be_confirmed(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/confirm-password', [
            'password' => 'password',
        ]);

        $response->assertRedirect();
        $response->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
    }

    public function test_password_is_not_confirmed_with_invalid_password(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/confirm-password', [
            'password' => 'wrong-password',
        ]);

        $response->assertSessionHasErrors();
    }
}

FILE: tests/Feature/Auth/PasswordResetTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Notifications\ResetPassword;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Notification;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PasswordResetTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_reset_password_link_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        $response = $this->get('/forgot-password');

        $response->assertStatus(200);
    }

    public function test_reset_password_link_can_be_requested(): void
    {
        Notification::fake();

        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/forgot-password', ['email' => $user->email]);

        Notification::assertSentTo($user, ResetPassword::class);
    }

    public function test_reset_password_screen_can_be_rendered(): void
    {
        Notification::fake();

        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/forgot-password', ['email' => $user->email]);

        Notification::assertSentTo($user, ResetPassword::class, function ($notification) {
            $response = $this->get('/reset-password/'.$notification->token);

            $response->assertStatus(200);

            return true;
        });
    }

    public function test_password_can_be_reset_with_valid_token(): void
    {
        Notification::fake();

        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->post('/forgot-password', ['email' => $user->email]);

        Notification::assertSentTo($user, ResetPassword::class, function ($notification) use ($user) {
            $response = $this->post('/reset-password', [
                'token' => $notification->token,
                'email' => $user->email,
                'password' => 'password',
                'password_confirmation' => 'password',
            ]);

            $response
                ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
                ->assertRedirect(route('login'));

            return true;
        });
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests/Feature/Auth/PasswordUpdateTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Auth;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PasswordUpdateTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_password_can_be_updated(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->from('/profile')
            ->put('/password', [
                'current_password' => 'password',
                'password' => 'new-password',
                'password_confirmation' => 'new-password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $this->assertTrue(Hash::check('new-password', $user->refresh()->password));
    }

    public function test_correct_password_must_be_provided_to_update_password(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->from('/profile')
            ->put('/password', [
                'current_password' => 'wrong-password',
                'password' => 'new-password',
                'password_confirmation' => 'new-password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasErrorsIn('updatePassword', 'current_password')
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');
    }
}

FILE: tests/Feature/OsisRoleTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Models\Osis;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class OsisRoleTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private function anggota(): Osis
    {
        return Osis::create([
            'id_osis' => 'OSIS-1',
            'nama' => 'Anggota Uji',
            'kelas' => 'XI',
            'jurusan' => 'RPL',
            'sekbid' => 'Akademik',
        ]);
    }

    public function test_guest_sees_members_on_landing_page(): void
    {
        $this->anggota();

        $this->get('/')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('Daftar Anggota OSIS')
            ->assertSee('Anggota Uji')
            ->assertSee('OSIS-1')
            ->assertDontSee('Tambah anggota')
            ->assertDontSee('Edit')
            ->assertDontSee('Hapus')
            ->assertDontSee('Login Admin')
            ->assertDontSee('Keluar');
    }

    public function test_old_member_list_redirects_to_landing_page(): void
    {
        $this->get('/osis')->assertRedirect('/#anggota');
    }

    public function test_guest_is_redirected_from_management_routes(): void
    {
        $this->get('/osis/create')->assertRedirect(route('login', absolute: false));

        $this->post('/osis', [
            'id_osis' => 'OSIS-2',
            'nama' => 'Anggota Baru',
            'kelas' => 'X',
            'jurusan' => 'RPL',
            'sekbid' => 'Seni',
        ])->assertRedirect(route('login', absolute: false));

        $this->put('/osis/1', [
            'id_osis' => 'OSIS-1',
            'nama' => 'Diubah',
            'kelas' => 'XII',
            'jurusan' => 'RPL',
            'sekbid' => 'Akademik',
        ])->assertRedirect(route('login', absolute: false));

        $this->delete('/osis/1')->assertRedirect(route('login', absolute: false));
    }

    public function test_admin_dashboard_shows_management_controls(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();
        $this->anggota();

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->get('/dashboard')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('Dashboard admin')
            ->assertSee('Anggota Uji')
            ->assertSee('Tambah anggota')
            ->assertSee('Edit')
            ->assertSee('Hapus');
    }

    public function test_user_is_redirected_away_from_dashboard(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->get('/dashboard')
            ->assertRedirect('/');
    }

    public function test_admin_dashboard_lists_all_members(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        for ($i = 1; $i <= 6; $i++) {
            Osis::create([
                'id_osis' => 'OSIS-'.$i,
                'nama' => 'Anggota '.$i,
                'kelas' => 'XI',
                'jurusan' => 'RPL',
                'sekbid' => 'Akademik',
            ]);
        }

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->get('/dashboard')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('Anggota 1')
            ->assertSee('Anggota 6')
            ->assertSee('Tambah anggota')
            ->assertSee('Edit')
            ->assertSee('Hapus');
    }

    public function test_non_admin_sees_read_only_members_on_landing_page(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();
        $this->anggota();

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->get('/')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('Anggota Uji')
            ->assertDontSee('Tambah anggota')
            ->assertDontSee('Edit')
            ->assertDontSee('Hapus');
    }

    public function test_user_cannot_open_create_page(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->get('/osis/create')
            ->assertForbidden()
            ->assertSee('Akses ditolak');
    }

    public function test_user_cannot_store_member(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->post('/osis', [
                'id_osis' => 'OSIS-2',
                'nama' => 'Anggota Baru',
                'kelas' => 'X',
                'jurusan' => 'RPL',
                'sekbid' => 'Seni',
            ])
            ->assertForbidden();

        $this->assertDatabaseCount('osis', 0);
    }

    public function test_user_cannot_update_member(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();
        $anggota = $this->anggota();

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->put("/osis/{$anggota->id}", [
                'id_osis' => 'OSIS-1',
                'nama' => 'Diubah',
                'kelas' => 'XII',
                'jurusan' => 'RPL',
                'sekbid' => 'Akademik',
            ])
            ->assertForbidden();

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('osis', [
            'id' => $anggota->id,
            'nama' => 'Anggota Uji',
        ]);
    }

    public function test_admin_can_store_member(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->post('/osis', [
                'id_osis' => 'OSIS-2',
                'nama' => 'Anggota Baru',
                'kelas' => 'X',
                'jurusan' => 'RPL',
                'sekbid' => 'Seni',
            ])
            ->assertRedirect('/dashboard')
            ->assertSessionHas('success');

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('osis', [
            'id_osis' => 'OSIS-2',
            'nama' => 'Anggota Baru',
        ]);
    }

    public function test_create_form_lists_sekbid_options(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->get('/osis/create')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('Keagamaan')
            ->assertSee('Kesehatan');
    }

    public function test_admin_must_choose_a_valid_sekbid(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->from('/osis/create')
            ->post('/osis', [
                'id_osis' => 'OSIS-3',
                'nama' => 'Anggota Baru',
                'kelas' => 'X',
                'jurusan' => 'RPL',
                'sekbid' => 'Sekbid Palsu',
            ])
            ->assertSessionHasErrors('sekbid');

        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('osis', ['id_osis' => 'OSIS-3']);
    }

    public function test_admin_can_update_member(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();
        $anggota = $this->anggota();

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->put("/osis/{$anggota->id}", [
                'id_osis' => 'OSIS-1',
                'nama' => 'Anggota Diubah',
                'kelas' => 'XII',
                'jurusan' => 'RPL',
                'sekbid' => 'Akademik',
            ])
            ->assertRedirect('/dashboard')
            ->assertSessionHas('success');

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('osis', [
            'id' => $anggota->id,
            'nama' => 'Anggota Diubah',
        ]);
    }

    public function test_admin_can_delete_member_from_dashboard(): void
    {
        $admin = User::factory()->admin()->create();
        $anggota = $this->anggota();

        $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->from('/dashboard')
            ->delete("/osis/{$anggota->id}")
            ->assertRedirect('/dashboard')
            ->assertSessionHas('success');

        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('osis', ['id' => $anggota->id]);
    }

    public function test_user_cannot_delete_member(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();
        $anggota = $this->anggota();

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->delete("/osis/{$anggota->id}")
            ->assertForbidden();

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('osis', ['id' => $anggota->id]);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: tests/Feature/ProfileTest.php
====================================================================================================
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class ProfileTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_profile_page_is_displayed(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->get('/profile');

        $response->assertOk();
    }

    public function test_profile_information_can_be_updated(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->patch('/profile', [
                'name' => 'Test User',
                'email' => 'test@example.com',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $user->refresh();

        $this->assertSame('Test User', $user->name);
        $this->assertSame('test@example.com', $user->email);
        $this->assertNull($user->email_verified_at);
    }

    public function test_email_verification_status_is_unchanged_when_the_email_address_is_unchanged(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->patch('/profile', [
                'name' => 'Test User',
                'email' => $user->email,
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $this->assertNotNull($user->refresh()->email_verified_at);
    }

    public function test_user_can_delete_their_account(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->delete('/profile', [
                'password' => 'password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasNoErrors()
            ->assertRedirect('/');

        $this->assertGuest();
        $this->assertNull($user->fresh());
    }

    public function test_correct_password_must_be_provided_to_delete_account(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();

        $response = $this
            ->actingAs($user)
            ->from('/profile')
            ->delete('/profile', [
                'password' => 'wrong-password',
            ]);

        $response
            ->assertSessionHasErrorsIn('userDeletion', 'password')
            ->assertRedirect('/profile');

        $this->assertNotNull($user->fresh());
    }
}
